   PL: Dziecko zawsze wybuchało gromkim śmiechem i jak nikt lubiło głupie żarty, ale nie teraz.

   limit: do 340 znaków
   EN: Perhaps treating others with kind smiles and friendship was how she chose to ease her job of taking lives.

4. [Narracja] 아이의 검은 빨라. 누구도 보지 못한 찰나의 순간, 이미 검은 적을 두동강으로 갈라 놓고 있지.

   EN: The Invitation flutters for a moment before shimmering as though it were surrounded by a heat haze—and the children are transported to an unfamiliar environment.
   PL:
87. [Narracja] 이제 이야기 속에 놓이게 될 아이들에게 남길만한 말은…
   limit: do 340 znaków
   EN: And to the children who will soon find themselves characters in a tale, I would say...
   PL:
88. [Narracja] 음, 그래. 아마 그 아이는 그렇게 말하겠지.
   limit: do 340 znaków
   EN: Ah yes, of course. <i>That</i> child would say...

89. [Narracja] 부디 당신의 책을 찾으실 수 있기를.
